+namespace {
+int mpiRank = 0;
+int mpiSize = 1;
+int localMpiRank = 0;
+
+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", mpiRank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(status));
+    }
+}
+
+size_t localBegin(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+size_t localEnd(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+
+void allGatherRows(void* data, size_t n, MPI_Datatype type, size_t elementSize) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(mpiSize), displacements(mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < mpiSize; ++r) {
+        const size_t begin = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        const size_t end = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((end - begin) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(begin * n);
+    }
+    const size_t begin = localBegin(n);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(static_cast<char*>(data) + begin * n * elementSize, counts[mpiRank], type,
+                   data, counts.data(), displacements.data(), type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The regular dense phases are kept resident on the accelerator.  MPI owns a
+// contiguous range of receiver rows; after each time step those rows are
+// exchanged so every rank has the history needed by its next local update.
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Vec3* trianglesCenters, int* tau, size_t n,
+                                 size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount || j >= n) return;
+    if (i == j) { tau[i * n + j] = 0; return; }
+    const Vec3 a = trianglesCenters[i];
+    const Vec3 b = trianglesCenters[j];
+    const val_t dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y, dz = a.z - b.z;
+    tau[i * n + j] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t t, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && factor > ZERO) {
+            const val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+            if (source > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char scratch[];
+    val_t* values = reinterpret_cast<val_t*>(scratch);
+    int* lags = reinterpret_cast<int*>(values + blockDim.x);
+    val_t best = ZERO;
+    int bestLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestLag = static_cast<int>(lag); }
+    }
+    values[threadIdx.x] = best;
+    lags[threadIdx.x] = bestLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            (values[threadIdx.x + stride] > values[threadIdx.x] ||
+             (values[threadIdx.x + stride] == values[threadIdx.x] &&
+              lags[threadIdx.x + stride] < lags[threadIdx.x]))) {
+            values[threadIdx.x] = values[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            lags[threadIdx.x] = lags[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(lags[0]);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(localBegin(n));
+    }

+    cudaCheck(cudaFree(dDistances), "freeing distances"); cudaCheck(cudaFree(dB), "freeing distance radiosity");

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;

+namespace {
+int mpiRank = 0;
+int mpiSize = 1;
+int localMpiRank = 0;
+
+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", mpiRank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(status));
+    }
+}
+
+size_t localBegin(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+size_t localEnd(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+
+void allGatherRows(void* data, size_t n, MPI_Datatype type, size_t elementSize) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(mpiSize), displacements(mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < mpiSize; ++r) {
+        const size_t begin = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        const size_t end = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((end - begin) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(begin * n);
+    }
+    const size_t begin = localBegin(n);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(static_cast<char*>(data) + begin * n * elementSize, counts[mpiRank], type,
+                   data, counts.data(), displacements.data(), type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The regular dense phases are kept resident on the accelerator.  MPI owns a
+// contiguous range of receiver rows; after each time step those rows are
+// exchanged so every rank has the history needed by its next local update.
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Vec3* trianglesCenters, int* tau, size_t n,
+                                 size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount || j >= n) return;
+    if (i == j) { tau[i * n + j] = 0; return; }
+    const Vec3 a = trianglesCenters[i];
+    const Vec3 b = trianglesCenters[j];
+    const val_t dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y, dz = a.z - b.z;
+    tau[i * n + j] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t t, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && factor > ZERO) {
+            const val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+            if (source > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char scratch[];
+    val_t* values = reinterpret_cast<val_t*>(scratch);
+    int* lags = reinterpret_cast<int*>(values + blockDim.x);
+    val_t best = ZERO;
+    int bestLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestLag = static_cast<int>(lag); }
+    }
+    values[threadIdx.x] = best;
+    lags[threadIdx.x] = bestLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            (values[threadIdx.x + stride] > values[threadIdx.x] ||
+             (values[threadIdx.x + stride] == values[threadIdx.x] &&
+              lags[threadIdx.x + stride] < lags[threadIdx.x]))) {
+            values[threadIdx.x] = values[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            lags[threadIdx.x] = lags[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(lags[0]);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;

+namespace {
+int mpiRank = 0;
+int mpiSize = 1;
+int localMpiRank = 0;
+
+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", mpiRank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(status));
+    }
+}
+
+size_t localBegin(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+size_t localEnd(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+
+void allGatherRows(void* data, size_t n, MPI_Datatype type, size_t elementSize) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(mpiSize), displacements(mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < mpiSize; ++r) {
+        const size_t begin = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        const size_t end = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((end - begin) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(begin * n);
+    }
+    const size_t begin = localBegin(n);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(static_cast<char*>(data) + begin * n * elementSize, counts[mpiRank], type,
+                   data, counts.data(), displacements.data(), type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The regular dense phases are kept resident on the accelerator.  MPI owns a
+// contiguous range of receiver rows; after each time step those rows are
+// exchanged so every rank has the history needed by its next local update.
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Vec3* trianglesCenters, int* tau, size_t n,
+                                 size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount || j >= n) return;
+    if (i == j) { tau[i * n + j] = 0; return; }
+    const Vec3 a = trianglesCenters[i];
+    const Vec3 b = trianglesCenters[j];
+    const val_t dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y, dz = a.z - b.z;
+    tau[i * n + j] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t t, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && factor > ZERO) {
+            const val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+            if (source > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char scratch[];
+    val_t* values = reinterpret_cast<val_t*>(scratch);
+    int* lags = reinterpret_cast<int*>(values + blockDim.x);
+    val_t best = ZERO;
+    int bestLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestLag = static_cast<int>(lag); }
+    }
+    values[threadIdx.x] = best;
+    lags[threadIdx.x] = bestLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            (values[threadIdx.x + stride] > values[threadIdx.x] ||
+             (values[threadIdx.x + stride] == values[threadIdx.x] &&
+              lags[threadIdx.x + stride] < lags[threadIdx.x]))) {
+            values[threadIdx.x] = values[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            lags[threadIdx.x] = lags[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(lags[0]);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;

+namespace {
+int mpiRank = 0;
+int mpiSize = 1;

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", mpiRank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(status));
+    }
+}
+
+size_t localBegin(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+size_t localEnd(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+
+void allGatherRows(void* data, size_t n, MPI_Datatype type, size_t elementSize) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(mpiSize), displacements(mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < mpiSize; ++r) {
+        const size_t begin = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        const size_t end = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((end - begin) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(begin * n);
+    }
+    const size_t begin = localBegin(n);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(static_cast<char*>(data) + begin * n * elementSize, counts[mpiRank], type,
+                   data, counts.data(), displacements.data(), type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The regular dense phases are kept resident on the accelerator.  MPI owns a
+// contiguous range of receiver rows; after each time step those rows are
+// exchanged so every rank has the history needed by its next local update.
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Vec3* trianglesCenters, int* tau, size_t n,
+                                 size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount || j >= n) return;
+    if (i == j) { tau[i * n + j] = 0; return; }
+    const Vec3 a = trianglesCenters[i];
+    const Vec3 b = trianglesCenters[j];
+    const val_t dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y, dz = a.z - b.z;
+    tau[i * n + j] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t t, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && factor > ZERO) {
+            const val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+            if (source > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char scratch[];
+    val_t* values = reinterpret_cast<val_t*>(scratch);
+    int* lags = reinterpret_cast<int*>(values + blockDim.x);
+    val_t best = ZERO;
+    int bestLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestLag = static_cast<int>(lag); }
+    }
+    values[threadIdx.x] = best;
+    lags[threadIdx.x] = bestLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            (values[threadIdx.x + stride] > values[threadIdx.x] ||
+             (values[threadIdx.x + stride] == values[threadIdx.x] &&
+              lags[threadIdx.x + stride] < lags[threadIdx.x]))) {
+            values[threadIdx.x] = values[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            lags[threadIdx.x] = lags[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(lags[0]);
+}
+

+    for (int r = 0; r < mpiSize; ++r) {
+        const size_t begin = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        const size_t end = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((end - begin) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(begin * n);
+    }
+    const size_t begin = localBegin(n);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(static_cast<char*>(data) + begin * n * elementSize, counts[mpiRank], type,
+                   data, counts.data(), displacements.data(), type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Vector and Triangle Types
 // ============================================================================
@@ -541,6 +575,71 @@
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The regular dense phases are kept resident on the accelerator.  MPI owns a
+// contiguous range of receiver rows; after each time step those rows are
+// exchanged so every rank has the history needed by its next local update.
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Vec3* trianglesCenters, int* tau, size_t n,
+                                 size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount || j >= n) return;
+    if (i == j) { tau[i * n + j] = 0; return; }
+    const Vec3 a = trianglesCenters[i];
+    const Vec3 b = trianglesCenters[j];
+    const val_t dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y, dz = a.z - b.z;
+    tau[i * n + j] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t t, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && factor > ZERO) {
+            const val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+            if (source > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char scratch[];
+    val_t* values = reinterpret_cast<val_t*>(scratch);
+    int* lags = reinterpret_cast<int*>(values + blockDim.x);
+    val_t best = ZERO;
+    int bestLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestLag = static_cast<int>(lag); }
+    }
+    values[threadIdx.x] = best;
+    lags[threadIdx.x] = bestLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            (values[threadIdx.x + stride] > values[threadIdx.x] ||
+             (values[threadIdx.x + stride] == values[threadIdx.x] &&
+              lags[threadIdx.x + stride] < lags[threadIdx.x]))) {
+            values[threadIdx.x] = values[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            lags[threadIdx.x] = lags[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(lags[0]);
+}
+

+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(status));

+// The regular dense phases are kept resident on the accelerator.  MPI owns a
+// contiguous range of receiver rows; after each time step those rows are
+// exchanged so every rank has the history needed by its next local update.
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Vec3* trianglesCenters, int* tau, size_t n,
+                                 size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount || j >= n) return;
+    if (i == j) { tau[i * n + j] = 0; return; }
+    const Vec3 a = trianglesCenters[i];
+    const Vec3 b = trianglesCenters[j];
+    const val_t dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y, dz = a.z - b.z;
+    tau[i * n + j] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t t, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && factor > ZERO) {
+            const val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+            if (source > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char scratch[];

+    }

+    lags[threadIdx.x] = bestLag;

+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            (values[threadIdx.x + stride] > values[threadIdx.x] ||
+             (values[threadIdx.x + stride] == values[threadIdx.x] &&
+              lags[threadIdx.x + stride] < lags[threadIdx.x]))) {
+            values[threadIdx.x] = values[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            lags[threadIdx.x] = lags[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(lags[0]);

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;

+namespace {
+int mpiRank = 0;
+int mpiSize = 1;
+int localMpiRank = 0;
+
+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", mpiRank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(status));
+    }
+}
+
+size_t localBegin(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+size_t localEnd(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+
+void allGatherRows(void* data, size_t n, MPI_Datatype type, size_t elementSize) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(mpiSize), displacements(mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < mpiSize; ++r) {
+        const size_t begin = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        const size_t end = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((end - begin) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(begin * n);
+    }
+    const size_t begin = localBegin(n);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(static_cast<char*>(data) + begin * n * elementSize, counts[mpiRank], type,
+                   data, counts.data(), displacements.data(), type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The regular dense phases are kept resident on the accelerator.  MPI owns a
+// contiguous range of receiver rows; after each time step those rows are
+// exchanged so every rank has the history needed by its next local update.
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Vec3* trianglesCenters, int* tau, size_t n,
+                                 size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount || j >= n) return;
+    if (i == j) { tau[i * n + j] = 0; return; }
+    const Vec3 a = trianglesCenters[i];
+    const Vec3 b = trianglesCenters[j];
+    const val_t dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y, dz = a.z - b.z;
+    tau[i * n + j] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t t, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && factor > ZERO) {
+            const val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+            if (source > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char scratch[];
+    val_t* values = reinterpret_cast<val_t*>(scratch);
+    int* lags = reinterpret_cast<int*>(values + blockDim.x);
+    val_t best = ZERO;
+    int bestLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestLag = static_cast<int>(lag); }
+    }
+    values[threadIdx.x] = best;
+    lags[threadIdx.x] = bestLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            (values[threadIdx.x + stride] > values[threadIdx.x] ||
+             (values[threadIdx.x + stride] == values[threadIdx.x] &&
+              lags[threadIdx.x + stride] < lags[threadIdx.x]))) {
+            values[threadIdx.x] = values[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            lags[threadIdx.x] = lags[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(lags[0]);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+    if (mpiRank == 0) printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);

+    if (mpiRank == 0) printf("Building octree...\n");

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;

+namespace {
+int mpiRank = 0;
+int mpiSize = 1;
+int localMpiRank = 0;
+
+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", mpiRank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(status));
+    }
+}
+
+size_t localBegin(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+size_t localEnd(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+
+void allGatherRows(void* data, size_t n, MPI_Datatype type, size_t elementSize) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(mpiSize), displacements(mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < mpiSize; ++r) {
+        const size_t begin = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        const size_t end = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((end - begin) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(begin * n);
+    }
+    const size_t begin = localBegin(n);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(static_cast<char*>(data) + begin * n * elementSize, counts[mpiRank], type,
+                   data, counts.data(), displacements.data(), type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };
 
+// The regular dense phases are kept resident on the accelerator.  MPI owns a
+// contiguous range of receiver rows; after each time step those rows are
+// exchanged so every rank has the history needed by its next local update.
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Vec3* trianglesCenters, int* tau, size_t n,
+                                 size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount || j >= n) return;
+    if (i == j) { tau[i * n + j] = 0; return; }
+    const Vec3 a = trianglesCenters[i];
+    const Vec3 b = trianglesCenters[j];
+    const val_t dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y, dz = a.z - b.z;
+    tau[i * n + j] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t t, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && factor > ZERO) {
+            const val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+            if (source > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char scratch[];
+    val_t* values = reinterpret_cast<val_t*>(scratch);
+    int* lags = reinterpret_cast<int*>(values + blockDim.x);
+    val_t best = ZERO;
+    int bestLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestLag = static_cast<int>(lag); }
+    }
+    values[threadIdx.x] = best;
+    lags[threadIdx.x] = bestLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            (values[threadIdx.x + stride] > values[threadIdx.x] ||
+             (values[threadIdx.x + stride] == values[threadIdx.x] &&
+              lags[threadIdx.x + stride] < lags[threadIdx.x]))) {
+            values[threadIdx.x] = values[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            lags[threadIdx.x] = lags[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(lags[0]);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

-    printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);
+    if (mpiRank == 0) printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);

+    if (mpiRank == 0) printf("Building octree...\n");
     state.octree.build(state.triangles);
 
     // Initialize areas
     state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);

+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)

codex
Implemented the unconditional hybrid accelerator path.

- [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-terra-medium_hybrid_r4/roomsim/roomsim.cpp) now:
  - initializes MPI and maps ranks to node-local CUDA devices;
  - partitions triangle rows across MPI ranks;
  - uses OpenMP for irregular form-factor/octree work;
  - uses CUDA kernels for delay calculation, wave propagation, and correlation;
  - synchronizes distributed radiosity rows with MPI each timestep.

- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-terra-medium_hybrid_r4/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt) now requires CUDA, MPI, and OpenMP and keeps the executable named `roomsim`.

Verified with a clean CMake build and a 2-rank CUDA/MPI validation run (`-n 20 -t 50 -v`), which passed.

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;

+namespace {
+int mpiRank = 0;
+int mpiSize = 1;
+int localMpiRank = 0;
+
+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA %s failed: %s\n", mpiRank, operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(status));
+    }
+}
+
+size_t localBegin(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+size_t localEnd(size_t n) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(mpiRank + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize); }
+
+void allGatherRows(void* data, size_t n, MPI_Datatype type, size_t elementSize) {
+    std::vector<int> counts(mpiSize), displacements(mpiSize);
+    for (int r = 0; r < mpiSize; ++r) {
+        const size_t begin = n * static_cast<size_t>(r) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        const size_t end = n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1) / static_cast<size_t>(mpiSize);
+        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((end - begin) * n);
+        displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(begin * n);
+    }
+    const size_t begin = localBegin(n);
+    MPI_Allgatherv(static_cast<char*>(data) + begin * n * elementSize, counts[mpiRank], type,
+                   data, counts.data(), displacements.data(), type, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+}

     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }

+// The regular dense phases are kept resident on the accelerator.  MPI owns a
+// contiguous range of receiver rows; after each time step those rows are
+// exchanged so every rank has the history needed by its next local update.
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Vec3* trianglesCenters, int* tau, size_t n,
+                                 size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount || j >= n) return;
+    if (i == j) { tau[i * n + j] = 0; return; }
+    const Vec3 a = trianglesCenters[i];
+    const Vec3 b = trianglesCenters[j];
+    const val_t dx = a.x - b.x, dy = a.y - b.y, dz = a.z - b.z;
+    tau[i * n + j] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* rho, const val_t* radE, val_t* radB,
+                                size_t n, size_t t, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        const int delay = tau[row + j];
+        const val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (static_cast<int>(t) >= delay && factor > ZERO) {
+            const val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+            if (source > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * source;
+        }
+    }
+    radB[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* distances, size_t n, size_t timesteps,
+                               size_t source, size_t firstRow, size_t rowCount) {
+    const size_t i = firstRow + blockIdx.x;
+    if (i >= firstRow + rowCount) return;
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char scratch[];
+    val_t* values = reinterpret_cast<val_t*>(scratch);
+    int* lags = reinterpret_cast<int*>(values + blockDim.x);
+    val_t best = ZERO;
+    int bestLag = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = threadIdx.x; lag < timesteps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[tt * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestLag = static_cast<int>(lag); }
+    }
+    values[threadIdx.x] = best;
+    lags[threadIdx.x] = bestLag;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride &&
+            (values[threadIdx.x + stride] > values[threadIdx.x] ||
+             (values[threadIdx.x + stride] == values[threadIdx.x] &&
+              lags[threadIdx.x + stride] < lags[threadIdx.x]))) {
+            values[threadIdx.x] = values[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            lags[threadIdx.x] = lags[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(lags[0]);
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================
